金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 9 月 14 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國 8 月 CPI 公布後，市場對聯準會政策路徑的關注進一步升溫：美伊衝突持續升溫帶動市
場對原油供給的擔憂，推升國際油價維持高檔震盪，能源價格上漲也使通膨再度成為市場關
注焦點。美國 8 月 CPI 年增率為 3.4%，與前月持平，但月增率由 0.1%升至 0.4%；核心 CPI
年增率則降至 2.4%。此次數據顯示通膨回落進程並未逆轉，但能源成本攀升仍可能增加未來
物價壓力。儘管聯準會理事表示，若通膨維持改善趨勢，9 月傾向按兵不動，但油價上漲是

否影響後續降通膨進程仍有待觀察，使利率政策路徑與市場預期持續出現分歧。
◼ AI 基建需求持續擴張，台灣 8 月出口再創歷史新高：台灣 8 月出口達 824 億美元、年增
41%，刷新歷史紀錄，其中電子零組件出口首度突破 300 億美元，年增 58%。AI 伺服器、
高階晶片、資料中心設備與雲端建設需求持續強勁，成為出口與科技產業成長的核心動能。
◼ 胡賽武裝威脅帶動西德州油價突破 100 美元大關：胡賽武裝 9 月 8 日攻擊沙國能源設施，9
月 10 日再奪取鄰近曼德海峽的摩卡港，推升能源風險溢價。由於沙國已將部分原油改經紅海
延布港出口，亞洲航線仍須通過曼德海峽。胡賽勢力逼近關鍵航道，即使尚未封鎖海峽，也可
能降低航商與保險業者承運意願，增添紅海供應中斷疑慮，帶動西德州油價突破 100 美元。
市場展望
◼ AI 題材擴散，科技股中期向上趨勢料不變：近期股市修正主要來自美債殖利率攀升壓縮高評
價科技股現值，半導體族群股價表現承壓；然而，AI 變現題材接棒，資金由晶片端轉向軟體應
用層，預料 AI 應用題材將持續發酵。建議降低高評價、高擁擠與高度依賴遠期獲利族群的集

中度，此外，投資人可採取定期定額或是趁股價拉回時分批佈局具獲利潛力的台日股市。
◼ 3～10 年期美國公債與投資級債為債市首選：美國財政部擴大長天期公債回購 ， 一度帶動長端
殖利率回落，但通膨與需求數據仍具韌性，且就業市場表現穩健，使年底前市場升息預期再度
升溫。市場對美國財政赤字、公債供給及長期通膨仍要求較高風險補償，考量當前 3～10 年
期美國公債與投資級債能鎖定目前較高息收 ， 且其受長端財政風險影響也相對較小 ， 故現階段
3～10 年期美國公債及投資級債的風險報酬結構相對較佳。

### Page 1

NYPCB

Alarm cleared with new drivers into 2027; 
re-it. Buy with PO raise to NT$2,000

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 2,000 TWD | Price: 1,340 TWD

BT substrate price hike resumed from 4Q26 
Following (see report) the worries of moderating BT substrate price hike in early 
September, we now expect NYPCB to still raise relevant ASP by single-digit % in 4Q26, 
and the hike is purely driven by favorable S/D which will fully translate into the accretion 
in bottom-line. Following this, BT substrate price will likely be raised by another 20+% in 
1Q27, due partly to the cost pass-through. We currently bake in ~10% revenue QoQ with 
32-33% GM for 4Q26, but we do not rule out the possibility for its revenue to surprise 
on the upside, should there be a faster ramp of its ABF substrate capacity in Kunshan. 
New driver for 2027: server CPU & mix optimization  
Looking into 2027 we expect the benefits of demand overflow on (see report) server 
CPU side to materialize, and we flag a chance for the firm to record 20+% revenue from 
the emerging business with AMD server CPU, where NYPCB is working on the 
qualification in its Shulin site currently and should capture most of the spillover demand 
if the progress goes smooth. Meanwhile, the firm will likely allocate more capacity from 
other sites (e.g. Kunshan) to fulfill the demand from Broadcom, which also helps enhance 
the mix. Currently we model ~50% GM for its ABF substrate business in 2027, but we 
see chance for further upward revision, driven by more favorable S/D dynamics and mix.  
Raise estimates/PO; prime beneficiary of price hike 
NYPCB’s share price (up ~50%) lags its major peers Unimicron/Kinsus (up ~70%) since 
late-July, but in our view it is still the best proxy to position for price hike in the 
substrate market. Judging from (see report) Kinsus’ August prints last week, we believe 
NYPCB remains on track to meet or beat the market’s expectations on N-T margins, and 
we reiterate Buy on the stock. Along with 13%/18% EPS raise in 2027/28E thanks to the 
more benign pricing/margin outlook driven by further mix improvement and S/D, we 
raise PO to NT$2,000 (from NT$1,700) same based on 35x 2H27-1H28E P/E.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Founded in 1997, NYPCB was listed on the Taiwan Stock 
Exchange in 2006. The firm is a 67%-owned subsidiary of 
Nan Ya Plastics Corporation (1303 TT). NYPCB's main 
products are HDI, PCB and IC substrates (including ABF and 
BT substrates), widely used in telecommunication, 
consumers, PC, and Auto applications. The clients include 
AMD, Broadcom, Samsung, Apple, Continental, etc. The 
main competitors include Unimicron, Kinsus, Ibiden, 
Shinko, and SEMCO, etc.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on NYPCB, in view of its continuous 
improvement of utilization rate and gross margin across 
ABF and BT substrate business into 2027, thanks to the 
robust demand growth from fabless customers and 
demand overflow in AI ASICs.

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
NYPCB (NANYF) 
Our PO of NT$2,000 is based on 35x 2H27-1H28E P/E. We view this multiple as fair 
given ROE and operating margin expansion to 39% and 41% in 2027-28E (vs 13% and 
14% in 2025-26E).

Upside risks to our PO are (1) tighter ABF supply, (2) higher SiP/AiP adoption, and (3) 
less intensified competition pressure in PCB business.

Downside risks are (1) greater ABF expansion from peers, (2) muted SiP/AiP adoption, 
and (3) more severe competition in PCB business.

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating. VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment rating s are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation).

Hon Hai (2317.TW): Sep beat: revenues +38% YoY; New AI servers, CPO 
switch and Foldable phones to drive 4Q26; Buy (on CL)
5 October 2026 | 11:23PM HKT
Hon Hai’s Sep revenues were +38% YoY/ 26% MoM to NT$1.2tn, or 15% / 21% 
higher than our / Bloomberg consensus estimates, reflecting strong AI servers ramp 
up and new smartphone models in contribution. From a MoM perspective, 
Consumer electronics is the strongest, followed by Components, Cloud and 
Networking and Computing, and all segments are positive MoM. The strong MoM 
reaffirms our positive view on the company’s leading market position across: (1) 
Smartphone: serving global leading brands, which suffer less under high memory 
costs, with new model launches and form factor changes (report link), driving the 
shipment and ASP increase, (2) AI servers: serving global leading CSP customers, 
and early in the delivery of the latest AI server racks, driving the shipment and ASP 
increase. The company is also the major supplier of CPO switch, supporting Sep and 
4Q26 growth. From a YoY perspective, Cloud and Networking is the strongest, 
followed by Components, Computing, and Consumer electronics, and all segments 
are positive YoY. We attribute the slower YoY in consumer electronics to its major 
brand customers’ fewer model launches this year, along with the new form factor 
(foldable phones) being not available in Sep, and a modest smartphone market 
under high memory costs.

3-month revenues preview: We expect company’s 4Q26E revenues to be +37% 
YoY/ +18% QoQ to NT$3.6 trillion. 3Q26 delivered strong revenues YoY growth of 
47%, or 5% / 7% ahead of our / Bloomberg consensus, and we expect to see both 
YoY and MoM growth throughout Oct-Dec, driven by new AI server racks, new 
smartphone models, and CPO switches. Management notes that AI-related products

server racks ramp up, Hon Hai’s leading market position in terms of scale, technology, 
and execution, and rising foldable phones to drive growth ahead. We keep 2027E/ 
2028E estimates largely unchanged.

Valuation: Our 12m TP for Hon Hai is unchanged at NT$400 and remains based on a 
P/E. Our target 2027E P/E multiple of 17.9x (unchanged) is derived from a peers’ 
average ratio of trading P/E to forward year NI YoY growth and OPM (P/E divided by the 
sum of NI YoY and OPM). Our target P/E of 17.9x is at the high-end of the company’s 
historical forward P/E, reflecting our positive view on Hon Hai’s business expansion from 
the competitive consumer electronics market to AI servers and EVs outsourcing, which 
should drive profit expansion with diversifying business opportunities for Hon Hai. 
Maintain Buy (on CL).

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on an 17.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design,

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 1

Summary: Winway reported September revenue of TWD1.25bn (-26.7% MoM; +87.7% 
YoY), brining 3Q26 revenue to a record high of TWD4.56bn (+29.3% QoQ; +152.6% YoY). 
The 3Q26 top-line result beat market consensus by 6.7% but missed Daiwa’s estimate by 
9.1%. Following August’s exceptionally strong top line (TWD1.71bn), we had factored in a 
higher shipment run-rate for September. However, the miss stems primarily from the 
lumpiness of probe-card deliveries (high concentration to a single customer) and customer 
qualification timelines, rather than order cancellations or share loss.

We forecast 4Q26 revenue to track flattish to slightly down QoQ, reflecting lower probe-
card shipment volumes compared to 3Q26, alongside typical year-end pull-in deceleration 
from customers. On profitability, we maintain our view that 2Q26 marked the trough for 
gross margin this year. As the revenue contribution from lower-margin MEMS probe cards 
declines, a more favourable product mix should drive a steady QoQ gross margin recovery, 
which may drive gross margin to reach 40% earlier than market expectations.

Although we remain positive on Winway’s structural growth profile, near-term catalysts 
appear muted. This reflects the revenue lumpiness in probe cards, while additional socket 
capacity will not come online until March 2027—pushing the corresponding revenue 
recognition into 2Q27. We will revisit our financial model upon the company’s 3Q26 results 
release. We currently have a Buy (1) rating on Winway with a 12-month TP of TWD10,000.

材料 *-KY (4763 TT)  2Q26 因山東廠停工歲修，毛利率降至  40.9% ， 3Q26 已回升至近  
50%；扣 除 匯 回 美 元 3 億 資 本 之 一 次 性 稅 款 約 新 台 幣 9.6 億元後 ，稅後 EPS 0.72 元 。
2027 年併入  Cerdia 全 球 產 能 後，年 產 能 達 絲 束 16.40 萬噸與醋片  19.50 萬噸 。全
球 絲 束 市 場 由 五 大 業 者 濃 縮 為 四 大 業 者 ， 公 司 採 取 控 制 接 單 策 略 ， 預計  10 ~11 /2026
進行  2027 年 合 約 調 價。

研 究 員 ： 吳 健 鉦   
A51755@cim.capital.com.tw

材料 *-KY 法 說 會 展 望 正 面， 4Q26 將 調 整 2027 年 合 約 價 格：  
材 料 *-KY 於  09/ 21 /2026 宣布以約美元  3.68 億 收 購 全 球 醋 酸 纖 維 大 廠  
Cerdia 100% 股 權 。 交 易 架 構 由 兩 部 分 構 成， 材料 -KY 以 自 有 資 金 支 付 現
金 對 價 約 美 元 2.14 億 ， 佔 比 約  58% ； 其 餘 發 行 新 股 109,295 仟股 ， 佔比
約  42% ， 交 易 完 成 後 賣 方 黑 石 集 團 實 體 將 持 有 材 料 -KY 增 資 後 約  9.95% 
股 權 ， 集 團 股 本 由 9.89 億 股 增 至 10.98 億股。   
被 收 購 方  Cerdia 2025 年 營 收 為 美 元  7.31 億 ， 調 整 後  EBITDA 為 美 元  
2.67 億， EBITDA 利潤率為  36.5% 。 截 至  06/2026 之近  12 個 月 ， 雙 方
合 計 營 收 約 達 10 億 美 元 ， 合 併 EBITDA 達 4 億 美 元 ， 合 併 EBITDA 利
潤率為  39% 。 此 交 易 使 材 料-KY 由 原 中 國 山 東 單 一 生 產 基 地 ， 擴 增 至 德 國
弗萊堡、美國金斯波特、巴西聖安德烈及俄羅斯謝爾普霍夫等全球五大基
地 。 合 併 後 全 球 醋 酸 纖 維 絲 束 設 計 產 能 達 16.40 萬噸 /年 ， 醋 酸 纖 維 素 醋 片
設 計 產 能 達 19.50 萬噸 /年。   
財 務 結 構 改 善 為 本 次 收 購 的 核 心 獲 利 驅 動 力 。Cerdia 目 前 帳 上 存 續 美 元 9 
億 私 債，到 期 日 為 2031 年，票 面 利 率 約 9%，年 利 息 支 出 達 美 元 0.81 億。
管 理 層 規 劃 於  2027 年 交 割 並 表 後 進 行 債 務 再 融 資 ， 透 過 台 系 與 歐 系 銀 行
將 利 率 降 至 4.5% –6% ， 預 估 每 年 節 省 約 美 元 0.41 億 利 息 支 出 ， 扣 稅 後 將
直 接 挹 注 合 併 淨 利 。 
材料 *-KY 2Q26 毛 利 率 降 至 40.9% ，主 因 山 東 各 廠 進 行 3 週至  1 個 月 歲
修 ， 停 工 固 定 費 用 全 數 列 入 當 期 銷 貨 成 本 ，3Q26 毛 利 率 已 回 升 至 近 五 成 ；
3Q26 另 認 列 自 山 東 匯 回 美 元 3 億 資 本 之 10% 一 次 性 所 得 稅， 約 為 0.30 
億美元 (約 新 台 幣 9.6 億元 )， 不 影 響 本 業 長 期 獲 利 。  
產業與產品布局上，全球絲束市場由五大業者濃縮為四大業者。 公司 採取
調控供給策略，將山東部分產能轉向長絲、短絲及不織布，維持絲束市場
供給緊縮以支撐產品價格。收購宣布後引發下游避險訂單，公司採取控制
接單策略，將談判遞延至  10/2026 至  11/2026 進行  2027 年 合 約 調 價 。
產品分工方面，材料 -KY 負 責 長 絲 、 短 絲 及 塑 膠 級 醋 片 ， 切 入 紡 織 、 眼 鏡
框與  AI 穿戴材料； Cerdia 則主打  85 種濾嘴規格及  100% 生物可降解
濾 嘴。材 料 *-KY 具 備 眼 鏡 膠 框 熱 解 回 收 專 利，正 與 東 莞 大 型 眼 鏡 廠 合 作 處
理每年約  1.65 萬 噸 廢 料 ， 預 計 12/2026 正 式 公 告 。

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係 「 首 次 」 給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時 ， 其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個
月目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為 四 個等級，定義如
上 。 爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」 或「區間操作」 之投資評
等，就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基
本 面 及 股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ，
研 判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。
我們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來
預測之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投
資 目 的 或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的 ，
既非對投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提
供詢價服務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值
及收益，可能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投
資 來 得 少 的 情 形 。

Growth in multiples
We forecast revenue to more than double next year
Bloom Energy’s planned capacity expansion of up to 10GW strengthens Kaori’s
long-term SOFC growth outlook, while higher liquid cooling content and
shipments should sustain liquid cooling momentum into 2027. CSP CDU heat
exchangers and semiconductor equipment cooling could provide additional

growth drivers from 4Q26. We raise our 2027/28 EPS forecasts by 14%/12% and
increase our target price to NT$2,710 from NT$2,300. Reiterate HC O-PF rating.
Bloom Energy to double its capacity
Bloom Energy (BE) has announced plans to acquire a 158k sqft facility in F remont,
U
S, comparable in size to its existing Fremont manufacturing plant, which can
produce up to 5GW. We believe the acquisition could support an eventual doubling
of its fuel cell stack manufacturing capacity to 10GW. We expect its supply chain

to support shipments of 2/4/6GW in 2026/27/28 to meet strong solid oxide fuel
cell (SOFC). demand. To support this ramp, Kaori is expanding capacity to 6-7x its
current level by end-2027. We now forecast Kaori’s sales from BE to grow by
185%/66% YoY in 2027/28, versus our previous estimate of 101%/77%.
Strong liquid cooling momentum into 2027
With SpaceX planning an additional 6-8GW of datacentre capacity in 2027, we
b
elieve GPU server suppliers Supermicro and Dell will be key beneficiaries,

supporting continued growth across their supply chains from 2H26 into 2027.
Higher content value for Rubin manifolds, together with stronger CDU and rear-
door heat exchanger (RDHx) shipments, should support Kaori’s liquid cooling
momentum. We forecast its liquid cooling revenue to grow by 60%/25% YoY in
2027/28.
Diversifying the growth engines
While SOFC and Supermicro-related liquid cooling remain Kaori’s main growth
d
rivers, we believe plate heat exchangers for CSP CDUs and liquid cooling

applications in semiconductor equipment could emerge as additional growth drivers
from 4Q26. These opportunities should broaden Kaori’s customer and application
exposure, supporting its longer-term growth outlook.
Earnings revisions and valuation
We raise our 2027/28SP EPS forecasts by 14%/12% to reflect higher expected
d
emand and shipments for BE's fuel cells, alongside a stronger liquid cooling
outlook. We increase our TP to NT$2,710 from NT$2,300, based on an unchanged

40x 2H27-1H28SP PE. Reiterate High Conviction Outperform rating.

Investment thesis
Kaori is well positioned to capitalise on rising demand for liquid cooling, and it
expects related product shipments to ramp up. The fuel-cell business should be a
huge growth driver in 2026-27 to meet AI power infrastructure demand in the US.
In the longer term, we believe the company will also benefit from heat-pump
demand as Europe is phasing out gas boilers, despite some noise about near-term
demand. Kaori has been planning capacity expansion earlier than peers to fully

capture demand, with rising exposure to large clients. We thus expect the company
to enjoy robust earnings in coming years.

6 October 2026 5
Catalysts
The major growth catalysts largely depend on fuel-cell projects for AI datacentres
taking off, increasing content value in liquid cooling offerings, and a clearer outlook
for hydrogen electrolyzer development.
Valuation details
We adopt a PE-based valuation methodology as we believe the market will mainly
focus on the earnings growth outlook, which is better reflected with PE metrics.

We base our valuation on 40x PE, which is around +1sd of its five-year average and
supported by an 93% EPS Cagr in 2026-28SP, to account for the surging demand
for AI server liquid cooling and power infrastructure. We are positive on the
company's long-term outlook given its growing stationary fuel cell demand from AI
datacentres, as well as its efforts to diversify customer exposure for liquid cooling
business. We believe the company can maintain its competitive edge over other

cooling players given its expertise in vacuum welding technology to manufacture
high-quality products.
Investment risks
Downside risks: 1) increased competition for Bloom Energy's fuel cell business or
new technology solutions replacing fuel cells; 2) slower kick-off of new fuel cell
projects for AI datacentres; 3) weaker-than-expected liquid cooling adoption; 4)
slower rollout of Europe/US government subsidies for heat pump installations.

to be below negative 10%. TSR is up/downside to 12-month target

Document 119: 9 月会合以降に日銀を取り巻く状況が変わった印象

Source PDF: all report pdf/9 月会合以降に日銀を取り巻く状況が変わった印象.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 7 日(水)

1/3 
9 月会合以降に日銀を取り巻く状況が変わった印象  
 経済調査部主席エコノミスト藤代宏一 
（℡：050-5474-6123）

・時事通信は10月６日付で「日銀が『基調的な物価上昇率』について、２％程度に到達したと
判断する方向で検討に入ることが５日、分かった」と報じた。記事内には10月29・30日開催
の金融政策決定会合で取りまとめる展望レポートで、そうした認識が記述されるとしてい
る。

・筆者はこの報道が現実になる可能性が高いと判断している。日銀は予てから基調的物価上昇
率が２％に到達する時期を「2026年度後半から2027年度」と予想してきたので（2026年度後
半にあたる）10月にそうした認識を示さないと、ハト派的な印象を与えてしまうことにな
る。

・既に日銀の政策態度は基調的物価上昇率を２％に「引き上げる」段階から、２％以上に「上
振れさせない」段階に移行している。局面が変化した理由としては、やはり原油高とそれに
対する企業の価格設定行動の積極化が大きいだろう。従来、原油高は企業収益を圧迫するこ
とで、賃上げを阻害し、個人消費の停滞に繋がってきた。そうした環境下で内需は拡大しな
いため、原油高は短期的にインフレ要因となっても、やや長い目でみるとデフレ的な色彩を
帯びていた。もっとも、2026年春先以降の原油高局面では、政府の手厚い支援もあり、企業
・日経平均株価は先行き12 ヶ月72,000 円程度で推移するだろう 
・USD/JPY は先行き12 ヶ月 155 円程度で推移するだろう 
・日銀は政策金利を12 月に 1.5％とした後、27 年末までに2.25％とするだろう 
・FED はFF 金利を、年内は4.0％で据え置くだろう

### Page 2

2/3 
は価格転嫁を積極的に進めた。企業物価指数は４月に前月比＋2.8％と即座に上昇し、前年比
伸び率は８月時点でも＋7.6％と高止まりしている。日銀短観（６月および９月調査）では１
年後の販売価格見通しが急伸した。こうした早期かつ大幅な価格転嫁の進展によって、企業
収益は傷みにくくなっている。このことは、名目賃金と名目消費が伸びやすい構図にあるこ
とを浮き彫りにしている。

・日銀が、基調的物価上昇率が２％に到達したとの認識を示すのであれば、それは利上げペー
スの加速を自ら正当化することになる。さすがに10月の利上げはビハインド・ザ・カーブに
陥ったことを認めたと市場参加者に受け止められる恐れがあるため、見送られると予想する
が、（前回利上げから約３ヶ月が経過する）12月会合では利上げが決定されるだろう。そう
なれば、３～４ヶ月に一度の利上げが標準ペースとなることが市場参加者に広く意識される
だろう。

・利上げを巡っては、経済指標に加えて「政府との距離感」を考慮する必要があり、予想は難
儀であった。しかしながら、ここ数週間で情勢は変化したように感じる。９月25日に片山さ
つき財務相が「官邸とも相談の上、私から初めて申し上げるが、先日の日米首脳会談では、
為替について、トランプ大統領から円安への懸念が示された」と発言したことを皮切りに、
同日には城内経済再生相が「アベノミクス的な意味でのリフレーション政策は、もうそうい
う局面は終わっている」とした。また10月５日に公表された９月30日の経済財政諮問会議の
議事要旨では、民間議員である若田部昌澄氏が「デフレではない状況になりつつある現状で
リフレ政策が終焉を迎えているというのは当然のことだ」との見解を示し、さらに９月の金
融政策決定会合で反対票を投じた佐藤綾野日銀政策委員は６日までに共同通信の単独インタ

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告

りません。 
3/3 
ビューに応じて「（政策金利を）段階的に調整していく方針には賛成している」とした。９
月18日会合における「主な意見」で内閣府の出席者（おそらく城内大臣）が利上げに消極的
と読める発言をしていたことと整合しないが、それは9月18日から25日の間に政府の態度が変
化した可能性が指摘できる。

・10月会合までに円安が進む、あるいは原油高等の要因によってＦｅｄが利上げに踏み切る事
態となれば、日銀も追随する可能性があるものの、そうした状況に至らなければ、10月は
「既往の利上げ効果を見極めたい」などとして利上げを見送るだろう。

・なお、これらに鑑みて、筆者は2027年７月の審議委員人事が中立ないしはタカ派的な人選に
なると予想する。高田委員と田村委員に考え方の近い人物に白羽の矢が立ち、リフレ派が送
り込まれることはないと考えている。利上げに積極的な２名の後任人事にリフレ派と目され
る人物が浮上した場合、金融市場では長期金利上昇や円安の反応が予想される。

Document 148: [402340] SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대하이닉스 자사주 소각_31fbe439

Source PDF: all report pdf/[402340] SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대하이닉스 자사주 소각_31fbe439.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-09-30
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

SK 스퀘어 (402340/KS) 
하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대 
예정보다 빠른 SK 하이닉스 자사주 매입 
SK스퀘어의 자회사인 SK하이닉스는 지난 8월 20일부터 11월 19일까지 자사주 40
조원(2,407만주, 3.3%)을 매입 예정이다. 9월 29일까지 1,715만주, 예정 매입수량 
대비 71.3%를 매입 완료하였다. 매입 초기 매일 65만주 매입하다가 9월 10일부터는 
60만주를 매입하고 있는데, 이러한 추세가 유지된다면 10월 중순경 자사주 매입이 마
무리될 예정이다. 자사주 매입 후 소각 예정으로 소각이 완료되면 SK스퀘어의 SK하아
닉스에 대한 지분율은 20.0%에서 20.7%로 상승하게 된다.

자사주 매입 이후 추가적인 주주환원에 대한 기대 
SK하이닉스는 지난 8월 19일 정책기간(‘25년~’27년) 동안 발생하는 누적 FCF의 
50% 이상을 주주환원 하는 주주환원 정책을 발표한 바 있다. 26년 SK하이닉스의 예
상 FCF는 164조원(컨센서스 기준)으로 50%를 주주환원 할 경우 자사주 매입 금액
(40 조원)을 제외한 주주환원 재원은 42 조원이다. 잔여 주주환원 재원이 모두 배당으
로 지급된다면 SK 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후 SK 스퀘어의 지분율은 20.7%이므로 
SK스퀘어의 배당수입도 8.7조원에 달한다. 지난 3월 SK스퀘어는 ‘26년~’28년 경
상배당수입의 30% 이상을 주주에게 환원하는 정책을 발표했다. 보수적으로 30%만 
가정하더라도 SK스퀘어의 배당재원은 2.6조원(현 시가총액 대비 1.8%)이다. SK스퀘
어는 연초 주주총회에서 배당가능이익 확보 차원에서 자본준비금 5 조 8,900억원을 
이익잉여금으로 전입한 바 있다. 이는 26년 재무제표가 확정되는 27년 3월 이후 배당
가능이익으로 편입되므로 SK스퀘어의 주주환원 확대가 기대된다.

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 185 만원(유지) 
SK스퀘어의 순자산 가치 중 98% 이상이 SK하이닉스 지분가치이다. SK하이닉스는 
자사주 매입 이후 17.7% 주가가 상승했으나, SK 스퀘어 주가는 11.4% 상승했다. SK
하이닉스 자사주 매입·소각은 SK 스퀘어의 보유 지분가치 상승뿐 아니라 지분율도 상
승하기 때문에 자사주가 소각될 경우 SK스퀘어의 주가가 탄력적으로 움직일 가능성이 
높다. SK스퀘어 현재 NAV 대비 할인율은 43.7%, 12MF PBR 1.4배로 7월 이후 주
가 조정으로 밸류에이션 매력도 높아졌다. 투자의견 매수와 목표주가 185만원을 유지
한다.

2026-09-30

지주회사/통신서비스/ESG. 최관순 / ks1.choi@sks.co.kr / 3773-8812

Document 238: First Impressions_ NZ business confidence, September 2026 (2026-09-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/First Impressions_ NZ business confidence, September 2026 (2026-09-01).pdf

### Page 1

#businessconditions #newzealand
First Impressions: NZ business confidence, September 2026
By Satish Ranchhod
Senior Economist, Westpac NZ
10:43 September 30 2026 Share
The recovery is continuing but it remains bumpy. Inflation gauges have not increased
despite the recent rise in oil prices.
Key results, September 2026
Business confidence: 51.9 (Prev: 53.7)
Expectations for own activity: 47.9 (Prev: 48.2)

### Page 2

Related articles
Activity vs same month one year ago: 10.8 (Prev: 16.4) 
Inflation expectations: 3.25% (Prev: 3.26%) 
Pricing intentions: 48.4 (Prev: 51.0)

There was a modest easing in September’s business confidence survey. However, gauges of past and
expected business activity, as well as business confidence remain at firm levels, consistent with the
recovery continuing in the latter part of the year.
While that’s welcome news, most of the responses were received prior to the recent spike in oil prices.
Those responses that came through later in the month were softer, but in most cases, we’re still left
with a relatively resilient picture of business conditions. Indeed, the ANZ noted that we haven’t seen the
same sort of pessimism we saw earlier in the year when fuel price first spiked. That’s consistent with
our recent theme of the economy shaking off the impacts of the Iran war.
There was one notable exception – hiring intentions did take a noticeable step down following the fuel
spike, and this will be a key area to watch over the coming months.
Notably, gauges of inflation pressure didn’t rise despite the recent fuel price spike. Expectations for
inflation were steady (3.25% this month vs 3.26% in August), while the number of firms planning on
raising prices fell back. That’s notable given the lift in wage and cost expectations. However, even with
today’s easing, the surveys measures of inflation pressure remain elevated.
Overall, we’re left with a picture of fairly resilient activity and contained (but not low) inflation pressures.
That very modest response to the fuel spike will help to calm nerves at the RBNZ ahead of October’s
policy review. We’ll we watching to see if sort of message is reflect in next week’s closely watch
Quarterly Survey of Business Opinion (out 6 October) and in the upcoming inflation reports (16 and 22
October).
Satish Ranchhod, Senior Economist

M: +64 21 710 852

Document 289: Ngân hàng TMCP Quân đội - Báo cáo cập nhật 2Q2026 (2026-09-18)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Ngân hàng TMCP Quân đội - Báo cáo cập nhật 2Q2026 (2026-09-18).pdf

### Page 1

Tăng trưởng tín dụng 2026 được dự 
báo khả quan so với cùng kỳ

NIM chịu áp lực trước mục tiêu tăng 
trưởng

Chất lượng tài sản suy giảm nhẹ do 
mặt bằng lãi suất ở mức cao

Khuyến nghị: MUA – Giá mục tiêu 
25,950 VNĐ/CP  
 Chúng tôi duy trì dự phóng tăng trưởng tín dụng 2026 đạt 27%, với động lực chính 
đến từ phân khúc KHDN, đặc biệt là từ nhóm FDI trong bối cảnh dòng vốn FDI vào 
Việt Nam và hoạt động xuất nhập khẩu duy trì tích cực. Xét về lĩnh vực, cho vay kinh 
doanh BĐS kỳ vọng sẽ tăng trưởng vượt trội khi ngân hàng tham gia vào thu xếp 
vốn cho các dự án hạ tầng và dự án BĐS ưu tiên, trong khi các mảng kinh doanh 
khác tăng trưởng ở mức khả quan.

Chúng tôi duy trì dự phóng NIM giảm so với năm 2025 do áp lực huy động khiến chi 
phí vốn duy trì ở mặt bằng cao, trong khi lãi suất cho vay dự kiến ở mức cạnh tranh 
để ưu tiên mục tiêu tăng trưởng tín dụng.

Chúng tôi dự phóng tỷ lệ nợ xấu của ngân hàng sẽ tăng 11bps YoY trong bối cảnh lãi 
suất đang neo ở mức cao và kỳ vọng ngân hàng sẽ tiếp tục duy trì chính sách trích 
lập dự phòng thận trọng nhằm củng cố bộ đệm, qua đó hoàn thành mục tiêu duy 
trì tỷ lệ bao phủ nợ xấu ở mức trên 100%.

Dựa trên triển vọng kinh doanh của ngân hàng cũng như đánh giá các yếu tố rủi ro, 
chúng tôi tiếp tục duy trì khuyến nghị MUA với cổ phiếu MBB với giá mục tiêu 
25,950 VNĐ/CP - tiềm năng tăng giá 26.2% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 17/09/2026.

Cơ cấu tín dụng  Hoạt động kinh doanh

Ngân hàng TMCP Quân đội (MBB) được 
thành lập năm 1994 với định hướng hoạt 
động ban đầu là cung cấp các dịch vụ tài 
chính cho các doanh nghiệp quân đội. Sau 
hơn 30 năm phát triển, MBB hiện đã trở 
thành ngân hàng sở hữu quy mô tài sản và 
tín dụng lớn nhất trong nhóm NH TMCP tư 
nhân. Phát triển theo mô hình tập đoàn, 
MBB đi đầu trong lĩnh vực chuyển đổi số với 
số lượng khách hàng và tỷ lệ CASA đầu 
ngành. 
Nguồn: MBB, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Tăng trưởng tín dụng có thể không đạt kế hoạch 30% do rào cản từ bài toán huy động, nhưng vẫn ở 
mức vượt trội so với ngành. 
 Tham khảo trong báo cáo kỳ trước tại đây 
Dự báo NIM giảm so với 2025, nhưng lợi nhuận vẫn tăng khả quan nhờ tăng trưởng tín dụng.  Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo

HOẠT ĐỘNG KINH DOANH 
Kết quả kinh doanh tích cực nhờ 
tăng trưởng thu nhập lãi thuần 
 Tín dụng trong 2Q2026 tăng mạnh so với quý trước, đạt 13.2% YTD cùng sự 
phục hồi của NIM đã giúp ngân hàng thiết lập kỷ lục mới về thu nhập lãi thuần 
với 16,894 tỷ đồng (+36.5% YoY). Thu nhập ngoài lãi tiếp tục thu hẹp trong quý 
này do hoạt động kinh doanh ngoại hối và chứng khoán kém tích cực, qua đó 
phần nào làm giảm tốc tăng trưởng tổng thu nhập hoạt động (+18.5% YoY). Chi 
phí dự phòng rủi ro đạt 4,247 tỷ đồng (-11.3% YoY), qua đó giúp LNTT tăng tích 
cực (+40.7% YoY), đạt 10,560 tỷ đồng. Lũy kế 1H2026, LNTT của ngân hàng đạt 
20,188 tỷ đồng, hoàn thành 51% kế hoạch BLĐ đề ra đầu năm.

### Page 5

5

Tăng trưởng tín dụng 2026 được dự 
báo khả quan so với cùng kỳ  
 Chúng tôi duy trì dự báo tăng trưởng tín dụng cả năm 2026 ở mức 27%, vượt 
trội hơn mặt bằng chung của ngành nhờ lợi thế tham gia nhận CGBB. Cụ thể: 
− Xét về phân khúc khách hàng, động lực chính kỳ vọng sẽ đến từ phân 
khúc KHDN, trong đó nổi bật là nhóm FDI khi ngân hàng đã chủ động 
thành lập khối Khách hàng FDI chuyên biệt trong bối cảnh dòng vốn 
FDI vào Việt Nam và hoạt động xuất nhập khẩu duy trì tích cực.  
− Xét về lĩnh vực, chúng tôi cho rằng cho vay kinh doanh BĐS sẽ tăng 
trưởng vượt trội với kỳ vọng ngân hàng tham gia vào thu xếp các dự án 
hạ tầng và dự án BĐS ưu tiên (BĐS KCN, NOXH). Ngân hàng cho biết tỷ 
trọng dư nợ mảng này dự kiến sẽ được duy trì trong biên độ mục tiêu 
13±2% tổng dư nợ tín dụng. Chúng tôi nghiêng về khả năng ngân hàng 
sẽ bám sát cận trên của mục tiêu (15%), nhằm tận dụng dư địa tăng 
trưởng từ các dự án ưu tiên, đồng thời duy trì kiểm soát chất lượng tài 
sản an toàn. Trong khi đó, dư nợ cho vay các mảng khác dự kiến sẽ ở 
mức thấp hơn tăng trưởng chung do môi trường vĩ mô còn nhiều khó 
khăn.

Biểu đồ 8. Cơ cấu tín dụng 
Tỷ trọng cho vay kinh doanh BĐS liên tục gia tăng 
trong các quý gần đây, trong khi cơ cấu cho vay 
thương mại và chế biến chế tạo có xu hướng giảm. 
Tính tới hết 1H2026, tăng trưởng cho vay kinh doanh 
BĐS, thương mại, và chế biến chế tạo lần lượt đạt 
38.2%, 4.9%, và 9.3% YTD.

Nguồn: MBB, KBSV

NIM chịu áp lực giảm so với 2025  Chúng tôi duy trì dự phóng NIM cả năm sẽ giảm so với 2025 (-15bps YoY) dựa 
trên luận điểm:  
(1) Ngân hàng sẽ tiếp tục dịch chuyển cơ cấu huy động sang các kênh có 
chi phí vốn (COF) cao hơn nhằm đáp ứng mục tiêu tăng trưởng tín 
dụng cao, trong bối cảnh cạnh tranh huy động trên thị trường 1 vẫn 
diễn ra gay gắt. Bên cạnh đó, ngân hàng cho biết dự kiến sẽ phát hành 
10-15 nghìn tỷ trái phiếu tier 2 trong 2H2026 với COF tương đối cao. 
(2) Với mục tiêu tăng trưởng tín dụng cao, chúng tôi cho rằng ngân hàng 
sẽ duy trì lãi suất cho vay ở mức cạnh tranh so với mặt bằng chung của 
ngành. Các khoản vay dự kiến vẫn sẽ tiếp tục tái định giá theo mặt 
bằng lãi suất hiện tại, tuy nhiên tốc độ sẽ chậm lại so với quý 2, do đó 
lợi suất IEA dự kiến sẽ tăng chậm hơn COF trong 2H2026.

Chất lượng tài sản suy giảm trong 
tầm kiểm soát  
 Trong 2Q2026, tỷ lệ hình thành nợ xấu ở mức 1.2% (-16bps QoQ), tương ứng 
4,142 tỷ đồng nợ xấu phát sinh mới, và tỷ lệ nợ xấu tăng nhẹ 3bps so với quý 
trước. Nợ xấu tăng trong quý này chủ yếu tới từ phân khúc bán lẻ (do hoạt động 
kinh doanh và giá đất của khách hàng tại địa phương bị ảnh hưởng sau khi sáp

nhập; và thanh khoản thị trường BĐS chậm lại) và một số KHDN trong lĩnh vực 
xây lắp. 
Cho cả năm 2026, chúng tôi dự phóng tỷ lệ nợ xấu của ngân hàng sẽ tăng 11bps 
YoY trong bối cảnh lãi suất đang neo ở mức cao. Cùng với đó, chúng tôi kỳ vọng 
ngân hàng sẽ tiếp tục duy trì chính sách trích lập dự phòng thận trọng nhằm 
củng cố bộ đệm, qua đó hoàn thành mục tiêu duy trì tỷ lệ bao phủ nợ xấu ở mức 
trên 100%.

mục tiêu 25,950 VNĐ/CP  
 Chúng tôi kết hợp 2 phương pháp định giá là P/B và Chiết khấu lợi nhuận thặng 
dư để tìm ra mức giá hợp lý cho cổ phiếu MBB. 
(1) Phương pháp định giá P/B 
Chúng tôi điều chỉnh P/B mục tiêu xuống mức 1.5x trong bối cảnh lãi 
suất neo cao và thanh khoản ngân hàng còn nhiều khó khăn. Dù vậy, 
P/B mục tiêu vẫn cao hơn so với P/B trung bình 5 năm của ngân 
hàng nhằm phản ánh kỳ vọng tích cực đối với việc room ngoại của 
ngân hàng được nới lên 49% theo Nghị định 69/2025/NĐ-CP. 
(2) Phương pháp chiết khấu lợi nhuận thặng dư  
Bên cạnh đó, chúng tôi kết hợp sử dụng thêm phương pháp chiết 
khấu lợi nhuận thặng dư để phản ánh rủi ro hệ thống và kỳ vọng dài 
hạn. 
Khuyến nghị MUA  với cổ phiếu MBB với giá mục tiêu 25,950 VNĐ/CP - tiềm 
năng tăng giá 26.2% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 17/09/2026.

### Page 1

Deutsche Bank Research Institute

### Page 2

Miha Hribernik | Mapping the Next Phase of China-US Ties |  September 2026
The Trump-Xi meeting in Washington met our key expectation: It delivered a subdued-yet-constructive summit resulting in a modest number of concrete deliverables that will minimise the near-term risks of abrupt
decoupling, a security crisis, or a disruptive trade war. This was the best plausible outcome for markets, given the structural nature of China-US competition. The brief two-month extension of the Busan agreement until

10 Jan 2027 will further delay new tariffs and export controls on both sides. It will also increase pressure to deliver a broader trade and investment package, and further trade-truce extensions, by the end of the year. We
anticipate more talks between US Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent and Chinese Vice Premier He Lifeng in Q4 as both sides seek a larger deal.
For China, stability with Washington will likely remain a priority as it manages rising trade tensions with the EU and prepares for top leadership reshuffles at its 2027 Party Congress. Combined with shifting US public

perceptions of China, these factors could create near-term space for more dealmaking on the sidelines of the upcoming APEC (18-19 Nov) and G20 (14-15 Dec) summits. As our surveys below show, US voters are now
more open to increased trade and investment with China, even at the cost of geopolitical concessions, while concerns over the cost of living have intensified. Both trends are now at their highest levels since April.
Nevertheless, political guardrails are not a panacea for a relationship that remains characterized by mistrust and competing national security priorities. As the next slide will show, the outlook for China-US relations

becomes more complex beyond this near-term horizon….
Tactical détente: The near-term outlook
Political commitment and shifting domestic priorities create space for stability into 2027

### Page 3

Miha Hribernik | Mapping the Next Phase of China-US Ties |  September 2026
An analysis of the geopolitical calendar reveals a complex slate of tariffs, import curbs, export controls, and sensitive pol itical events. Even as leader-level diplomacy has reduced the risk of a near-term breakdown, it
has done little to stem the tide of new bilateral economic restrictions. These have proliferated during 2026 to include new e xport controls, sanctions, and tariffs (see list), with significant implications for high-growth

industries including drones, AI, electric vehicles, and robotics.
Competition is unlikely to slow, and events could repeatedly test the fragile trade truce. Q4 2026 will see further China-US dialogue and China-EU trade talks. Should talks with Brussels fall short of averting increased
European trade pressure, Beijing may further prioritise near-term US stability. But the coming months will also bring challenges in the form of new US overcapacity tariffs and polysilicon levies, while next year will usher

in curbs on critical minerals and additional semiconductor tariffs. The run-up to the 10 Jan Busan truce expiry could also bring new economic restrictions as both sides seek negotiating leverage. Both countries will also
hold major political events (China’s Party Congress in 2027 and US elections in 2028), with potential implications for bilateral ties, while Taiwan’s Jan 2028 election will be another key watchpoint.
But these are just the events whose outcomes and impacts we can estimate in advance. Understanding probable China-US developments that are not on the calendar is equally important for investors. We should

assume that competitive dynamics across myriad policy issues will drive other developments and policy changes, often announce d with little or no advance notice. We examine some of the key issues in the next slide.
The policy horizon: Key events to watch (2026–2029)
The next several years will see more summits, tariffs, major political events, and key elections

### Page 4

Miha Hribernik | Mapping the Next Phase of China-US Ties |  September 2026
Unless consistently managed, structural competition could move markets and test stability through the end of the
decade and beyond. We highlight five watchpoints for markets:
1. The AI race: Dialogue is set to strengthen AI safety guardrails, but national and economic security imperatives
mean it is unlikely to significantly slow the China-US AI race, much less meaningfully reverse existing restrictions.

Any attempts to pace AI development would touch on questions of tech restrictions, including Beijing’s perceptions
of US technological containment, which could increase amid potential restrictions on remote access to AI chips and
Chinese data centre components.
2. Tariff pressures: Trade tensions have subsided given a lower US trade deficit and the new Board of Trade, which is
set to reduce tariffs on US$60 billion of non-sensitive goods. But significant differences persist: Washington plans

to unveil new polysilicon and semiconductor tariffs, as well as overcapacity levies on 16 economies (including
China). The US is also seeking to restrict Chinese inputs in North American supply chains and target alleged
transshipment, while Beijing shores up its supply chain dominance in areas of US import dependence, such as
advanced drone components and processed critical minerals – which continue to be a source of friction.
3. Regulatory toolkits: The wider tech sector remains central to expanding export controls and restrictions. China is

strengthening its toolkit. Its 2026 codification of anti-derisking regulations strengthens Beijing’s ability to take
action over foreign sanctions or export controls. Meanwhile, its outbound investment regulations, effective 1 Jul,
cover technology, data, talent and capital. In the US, Congress is drafting legislation to change outbound
investment reporting requirements for biotech and biopharma, restrict government procurement of humanoid
robots and solar equipment, and ban the use of China-linked connected vehicles on military installations.

4. Geopolitical stress-tests: The war in Iran is unlikely to derail relations in the near term, but the conflict has strained
bilateral ties. Any potential broadening of the conflict in scale and intensity, or a further expansion of US sanctions
that impact Chinese interests, could test guardrails. Additionally, while the probability of conflict in the Taiwan
Strait remains low, differences over Taiwan could remain unresolved across successive administrations in both

Washington and Beijing into the 2030s and beyond.
5. Political anchors: Two domestic events will shape relations into the 2030s. Beijing’s priority is stability ahead of the
late 2027 Party Congress. For China, dialogue can help reduce the risk of costly escalation, potentially slow the
pace of new US economic restrictions, and buy time for efforts to strengthen self-reliance in strategic sectors such
as semiconductors (where China is aiming for 80% self-sufficiency by 2030).

In the US, a durable bipartisan consensus on China could make visible concessions to Beijing increasingly difficult
ahead of the 2028 presidential election. Recent bipartisan criticism of the Trump administration’s decision to allow
limited Nvidia H200 chip sales to China reflects the political constraints facing a transactional approach to China,
raising the prospect that guardrails will strain closer to election day.
Structural strains: Testing the guardrails

Navigating the trade, technology, and regional pressure points

Document 323: The hobby economy and the price of fun Consumers are paying more for fun as Millennials and Gen Z reshape how they spend their leisure time. 2 min read

Source PDF: all report pdf/The hobby economy and the price of fun Consumers are paying more for fun as Millennials and Gen Z reshape how they spend their leisure time. 2 min read.pdf

Economy 
The hobby economy and the price of fun 
23 September 2026

Key takeaways 
• Consumers are spending more on hobbies. Hobby spending rose 7.9% year-over-year (YoY) in August, but this was more than 
double the pace of transaction growth, suggesting the reemergence of "funflation" in hobbies, according to Bank of America 
card data. 
• Older Millennials are the biggest hobby spenders, according to Bank of America card data, despite having the least amount of 
leisure time of any generation. They also have the highest share of the population with hobby spending, though this likely 
reflects both personal interests and spending on their kids. 
• Gen Z's hobby habits are evolving. While hobby purchases have slowed sharply, younger consumers continue to devote 
significant leisure time to gaming, with over a quarter of Gen Z having some type of online or video game spending, according 
to Bank of America card data.

Spending grows over twice as fast as transactions in August 
Bank of America card data suggests we’re seeing some “funflation” (the rising price of fun) in hobbies – defined here as arts and 
crafts stores, hobby shops and outdoor recreation service providers and retailers that specialize in activities like hiking, camping, 
skiing and scuba diving (see Methodology).  
Using our definition, spending on hobbies grew 7.9% year-over-year (YoY) in August 2026. This was over twice as fast as 
transaction growth, which was up a solid 3.4% YoY (Exhibit 1). This is the opposite of last August, when transactions outpaced 
spending by nearly a percentage point. It’s also a stark contrast from 2023, when spending fell nearly 4% YoY and transactions 
were up almost 1%.  
Exhibit 1: Spending grew at twice the rate of transactions in August 2026 
Spending and transaction growth per household on hobbies (three-month moving average, YoY%)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Looking across a longer period, we see that spending growth also outpaced that of transactions throughout the pandemic 
(Exhibit 2). Transactions began declining in 2021, with spending following suit in mid-2022. In fact, spending declined faster 
than transactions from 2023 until late 2024, with both improving the following year.

In our view, it’s likely that people splurged on hobbies amid social distancing during the pandemic, then gravitated back toward 
pricier alternatives like travel throughout 2022 and 2023. However, last year there may have been some rotation back to less 
expensive leisure like hobbies as after-tax wage growth slowed considerably (read more in Consumer Checkpoint: Still sizzling). 
Bank of America card data suggests there’s been a recent acceleration in hobby spending in the past few months, possibly as 
some consumers balance shift away from travel due to higher prices from rising fuel costs. 
Exhibit 2: Spending growth outpaces transaction growth, while the opposite was true from mid-2022 until the end of 2024

Source: Bank of America internal data

Older Millennials claim the hobbies crown, while Gen Z lags behind 
Looking across generations, Bank of America card data suggests that older Millennials spend the most per customer on hobbies, 
followed closely by Baby Boomers and Gen X, while Gen Z spends the least (Exhibit 3).  
Exhibit 3: Older Millennials spend the most on hobbies, followed by Baby Boomers and Gen X 
Average spending per customer by age generation (three-month moving average to August 2026, index Gen Z = 100)

BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Older Millennials also boast the largest share of their population with hobby spending (Exhibit 4 and Exhibit 5). In our view, it’s 
likely because this group is most likely to have young children. Which may mean older Millennials not only spend on their own 
hobbies but on their kids’ as well.

The idea that older Millennials are likely spending on themselves and their kids is supported by data from the American Time Use 
Survey (ATUS) conducted by the US Census Bureau. That survey suggests that older Millennials have the least amount of leisure 
time, with only around four hours and fifteen minutes on average per day (Exhibit 6). Meanwhile, the two groups between ages 
“15 and 24” and “55 and 64” have nearly five and a half hours per day, with those 65 and older boasting over seven hours of 
leisure time per day.   
On the other hand, younger Millennials and Gen Z have the lowest average spending, but a higher share of their population with 
hobby outlays. To us, this suggests that these generations are likely gravitating most of their spending toward less expensive 
hobbies like arts, crafts and board games.

Gen Z consumers slow hobby purchases… 
Additionally, while younger and older Millennials as well as Gen X appear to be ramping up hobby spending, Baby Boomers and 
Gen Z are slowing theirs (Exhibit 7). Notably though, Gen Z’s slowdown in purchases has been stark. Transaction growth was near 
zero in August 2026, a dramatic slowdown compared to the nearly 16% YoY increase the prior year, according to Bank of 
America card data (Exhibit 8).

This slowdown was driven by a significant decrease in spending on outdoor recreation, offsetting increases in arts and crafts 
retailers and hobby shops. In our view, the prior year’s transactions may have been boosted by purchases of longer-lasting items, 
such as hiking boots, which don’t need to be bought as frequently. It’s also possible that this reflects a shift to more “granny 
core” activities like knitting, sewing, or baking (arts and crafts). 
…and ramp up their gaming spend

games. The social component to these activities can’t be overlooked as a factor. In fact, over a quarter of Gen Z said they feel a

and half times as high as younger Millennials and over eight times the rate of Baby Boomers.

Methodology  
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Any payments data represents aggregated spend from US Retail, Preferred, Small Business and Wealth Management clients with a deposit 
account or credit card. Aggregated spend include total credit card, debit card, ACH, wires, bill pay, business/peer-to-peer, cash, and checks.  
Any Small Business payments data represents aggregate spend from Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit 
card. Payroll payments data include channels such as ACH (automated clearing house), bill pay, checks and wire. Bank of America per Small 
Business client data represents activity spending from active Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit card and 
at least one transaction in each month. Small businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America and generally defined as 
under $5mm in annual sales revenue.  
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
The differences between the total and per household card spending growth rate (if discussed) can be explained by the following reasons:  
1. Overall total card spending growth is partially boosted by the growth in the number of active cardholders in our sample. This could be due to 
an increasing customer base or inactive customers using their cards more frequently. 
2. Per household card spending growth only looks at households that complete at least five transactions with Bank of America cards in the 
month. Per household spending growth isolates impacts from a changing sample size, which could be unrelated to underlying economic 
momentum, and potential spending volatility from less active users. 
3. Overall total card spending includes small business card spending while per household card spending does not. 
4. Differences due to using processing dates (total card spending) versus transaction date (per household card spending). 
5. Other differences including household formations due to young adults moving in and out of their parent’s houses during COVID. 
Any household consumer deposit data based on Bank of America internal data is derived by anonymizing and aggregating data from Bank of 
America consumer deposit accounts in the US and analyzing that data at a highly aggregated level. Whenever median household savings and 
checking balances are quoted, the data is based on a fixed cohort of households that had a consumer deposit account (checking and/or savings 
account) for all months from January 2019 through the most current month of data shown.  
Bank of America aggregated credit/debit card spending per household includes spending from active US households only. Only consumer card 
holders making a minimum of five transactions a month are included in the dataset. Spending from corporate cards are excluded. Data 
regarding merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial 
services companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System 
(NAICS), which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs.  
Hobby spending includes spending across all payment vehicles at arts and crafts stores, hobby shops, ski resorts, scuba retailers and rentals, 
adventure activities, educational toys, outdoor retailers and some toy stores. Not included in this analysis are some hobbies like golf or travel, 
due to further analysis in other pieces. Also not included are more digital forms of entertainment like video games, though this category is 
included as a separate analysis.   
Includes a reference to Bank of America’s Navigator Survey’s question “Outside of your family, in which places or groups do you currently feel a 
sense of community or belonging? (Select all that apply).”  The survey was conducted between January 16 and 22, 2026 and included 2,041 
participants.  
Lower, middle and higher household income cuts in Bank of America credit and debit card spending per household, and consumer deposit 
account data are based on quantitative estimates of each households’ income. These quantitative estimates are bucketed according to terciles, 
with a third of households placed in each tercile periodically. The lowest tercile represents ‘lower income’, the middle tercile represents ‘middle 
income’ and the highest tercile ‘higher income’. The income thresholds between these terciles will move over time, reflecting any number of 
factors that impact income, including general wage inflation, changes in social security payments and individual households’ income. The 
income and tercile in which a household is categorised are periodically re-assessed.  
Generations, if discussed, are defined as follows: Gen Z, born after 1995; Younger Millennials: born between 1989-1995; Older Millennials: born 
between 1978-1988; Gen Xers: born between 1965-1977; Baby Boomer: 1946-1964; Traditionalists: pre-1946. Our Gen Z analysis only includes 
spending by people that are 18 years or older and likely reflects the trends from the members of this generation who are living on their own.  
Any reference to card spending per household on gasoline includes all purchases at gasoline stations and might include purchases of non-gas 
items. 
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

Document 328: The Renminbi Appears to be Undervalued by 30 Percent (2026-09-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/The Renminbi Appears to be Undervalued by 30 Percent (2026-09-01).pdf
Report source: CFR (Follow the Money)
Date: 2026-09-01
Pages: 14
Status: ok

### Page 1

1
EXPERT TAKE FOLLOW THE MONEY
The Renminbi Appears to be Undervalued by 30
Percent
China’s official current account data understate the scale of its external surplus
and, by extension, the degree of renminbi undervaluation.
PUBLISHED
Articles Follow the Money Share
Log In
Regions Topics Research Experts Events The Future of American Strategy

### Page 2

2
September 1, 2026 3:07 p.m.
Brad W. Setser CFR EXPERT
Whitney Shepardson Senior Fellow
Harvard’s Dr. Gopinath (formerly of the IMF) asked
about the basis for my estimate (in Greg Ip’s excellent
WSJ column) that the RMB is now undervalued by
30 percent.*
The simple answer: I used the IMF’s own current
account methodology but didn’t take China’s reported
current account surplus at face value. I believe that
the surplus is significantly understated, as it doesn’t

map to other known numbers about China’s economy
and trade performance.**

### Page 3

3
The IMF’s current account gap methodology hinges
on the difference between the observed current
account and the “current account” norm calculated by
the IMF (on the basis of demographics, levels of
development, the net international investment
position, and other variables). For China, that current
account norm is now estimated at 0.6 percent of
GDP (2026 ESR, Table 2.6)
A cyclical adjustment added 0.2 percent of GDP to
the estimated norm for 2025. The end-2025 surplus

was 3.8 percent of GDP. The 3 percent of GDP gap
resulted in an estimated undervaluation of 21 percent
as the IMF’s work implies that a 7 percent move in
the exchange rate (or equivalent changes in other
policies) reduces the surplus by a percentage point of
GDP.
A bigger surplus thus implies a bigger “gap” and a
bigger estimated undervaluation (for a given
constellation of policies).
And I think the 3.8 percent of GDP surplus for end-
2025, now up to 4 percent of GDP in the last four

quarters of data, is understated. I am not the only one
who thinks by the way; the raw customs data—
adjusted for the observed deficit in travel services and
the recent surge in gold imports—implies an overall
surplus of close to 6 percent of GDP these days.

### Page 4

4
There are three reasons to believe that the reported
current account surplus understates the scale of
China’s real surplus:
1. The reported investment income deficit makes no
economic sense.
The reported deficit on investment income is very
hard to understand. You don’t have to take my word
on this. The IMF looked into China’s investment
income balance and agreed (2025 ESR, Box 1.1). So
did Ma and Wei.
The fact that China reports an investment income

deficit of over $100 billion with net foreign assets of
over $4 trillion isn’t easily explained. Yes, there can be
a difference in return on foreign direct investment in
China and Chinese direct investment abroad. But
with roughly equal levels of investment in both

### Page 5

5
directions, that gap in returns on direct investment
would need to be between 6 and 8 percentage points
to offset the $120-160 billion in interest income that
would be expected on China’s net holdings of foreign
bonds and other interest-paying assets at a
conservative 3 to 4 percent interest rate (CDB and
state commercial bank loans, if anything, should carry
a higher rate, as the CDB lends at a big markup over
the U.S. policy rate).
And there is no real evidence that foreign direct

investors are earning a ton of money inside China
these days. The auto companies certainly are not.
Many have abandoned their Chinese JVs, or are using
them primarily to supply global markets. The
exception—Apple—actually proves the rule. Apple
does have a nice business in China. But China’s 2022
statistical adjustments should move Apple’s returns
out of the “direct investment” line as Apple’s IP
returns accrue to its Irish subsidiary, not its Chinese

subsidiary, and into the balance of payments as a small
goods deficit.***
The evolution of the investment income line item
also doesn’t make any sense. The investment income
deficit increased a lot in 2019 and 2020. Certainly,
interest rates were low globally in 2020. But that
doesn’t explain why the investment income balance
remained in a large deficit even after global interest
rates soared. The returns on China’s large and growing

### Page 6

6
external loan and bond portfolio should have soared,
but they didn’t. That makes no sense.
China in Q1 reported something like a 2 percent
return on its foreign bonds and overseas lending. That
doesn’t compute, as SAFE eked out a 3 percent
return in the low-for-long era after the GFC and can
get more than that from a portfolio of five-year
Treasury notes and five-year bunds.****
2. China started importing a ton of gold in 2026.
China’s gold imports have gone from around 0.5

percent of GDP to 1.5 percent of GDP in a couple of
quarters. That is strange—especially as China’s rising
interest in gold came as gold prices fell.
Gold is a strange import. If the gold isn’t going
directly to the central bank, it counts as an import
even though it is often held as a financial asset. And if
the gold is going directly to the central bank, it is
counted as the purchase of a financial asset. Some
even think SAFE is buying gold through domestic

brokers as a means of shrinking the reported surplus.
It thus isn’t really controversial to move the gold
import line out of the current account and over to the
financial account.
3. China started fudging its balance of payments
goods number when it changed its balance of
payments methodology back in 2022.

### Page 7

7
That change is now statistically faded into the 2021
data as well.
The changes were in theory an attempt to implement
the IMF’s latest guidance (BPM6, which the U.S.
hasn’t done). While the IMF has recognized
problems with China’s reported investment income
deficit, the IMF statistics department hasn’t given an
inch on the new goods data, and argues that China,
unlike the U.S., has at least tried to follow its
statistical guidance.
But China’s actual implementation of the IMF’s

guidance was suspect to say the least. I covered this
debate in detail a year ago (Apple plays a large role in
China’s argument). And the IMF refuses to assess
whether the results of the new methodology are really
an improvement over the results of the old
methodology.

### Page 8

8
One of the adjustments that SAFE outlined to the
IMF back in 2024—the “in China for China”
adjustment for firms that don’t have an onshore JV
and rely on contract manufacturing—is in principle
reasonable. Moving Apple’s profit out of income over
to the BOP goods line would be consistent with
Apple’s treatment in the Irish balance of payments
data, but it should have produced a $20-$30 billion
change in the goods number, not a $300 billion
change. And moving Apple’s “in China” profit from

one line of the current account to another shouldn’t
have changed the overall current account numbers.
The other adjustment, which arbitrarily reduces
exports in the balance of payments down relative to
customs on a never demonstrated claim that contract
manufacturers were inflating their exports in the
customs data by using a firm’s global wholesale price
in customs rather than the amount actually received,
simply doesn’t check out. The IMF, to the best of my

knowledge, has never looked into the details here
either.
It is relatively easy to construct a consistent time
series using China’s pre-2021/22 methodology for
calculating its balance of payments goods data (the
standard adjustment moves insurance and freight
charges over to services). The new methodology, at
least until recently, really did lower the goods surplus,
and thus the overall current account surplus.

### Page 9

9
There was clearly a massive downward adjustment to
balance of payments goods for a while, without
offsetting changes in other lines in the current
account.
I was ginned up about this for a while. It really had a
big impact on the 2023 and 2024 current account
surplus numbers and thus changed the IMF’s overall
assessment of China. But—for complex reasons—the
impact of this adjustment has shrunk, and it is now
less significant than the investment income and gold

distortions.***** See the grey and gold bars in the
chart below.
When these factors are taken into account, I estimate
that China’s surplus should be around 5.5 percent of
GDP—which, without the cyclical adjustment, works
out to a 35 percent undervaluation (33 percent, with
the cyclical adjustment).

### Page 10

10
I also used the IMF’s methodology to estimate what
the undervaluation should have been in the past
(without the cyclical adjustment, as I cannot estimate
that reliably).
The larger estimated undervaluation checks out
against a range of other indicators.
The Big Mac Index (37 percent undervaluation).
Industry-specific cost comparisons. Greg Ip has a
nice chart from Goldman. European auto analysts
think China has a 30 percent edge in production
costs.

It is also clear in China’s export outperformance
relative to global trade. And the breadth of this
outperformance.
At the end of the day though, it doesn’t currently
matter too much whether the renminbi is

### Page 11

11
undervalued by 20 percent or 35 percent. There is
consensus that there is a significant undervaluation.
Fixing that is, by far, the easiest way to address
concerns about China’s ever-rising trade surplus—and
to get Xi to reconsider China’s growth model and do
more to support internal demand.
* Dr. Gopinath observes that the IMF’s current
account gap can be closed through a combination of
exchange rate moves, domestic policy changes and
changes in the international environment (i.e. slower

demand growth abroad, slower growth in global
trade), not just through exchange rate changes. That is
a fair point. Though I would note that there isn’t
much evidence over the last 15 years that Chinese
demand growth spills over into import demand.
Think of an industrial policy for everything. Or, more

### Page 12

12
formally, income elasticity that is way below one.
That implies the exchange rate, which does seem to
impact export performance more than the IMF
believed in the past, will need to do much of the work.
** The customs surplus can (and has been) checked
against counterparty data. The travel deficit now
broadly maps to observed tourism flows. And the
reported NIIP is smaller than it should be given
China’s accumulated surpluses. The gross asset and

liability position in the IIP data can nonetheless be
used to generate an independent estimate of
investment income (part of the current account).
*** I agree, for what it is worth. My concern is that
China never showed its work, and there isn’t any
evidence that moving Apple’s “in China” return over
to the BOP goods line items was matched by
offsetting adjustments to either the services line (if
the “in China” profit was previously counted as a
royalty to Apple Ireland) or the income line (if it was

previously added to the income line, adding to
China’s deficit in FDI income). The current account
impact of moving around Apple’s $20 to 30 billion
“in China” profit should be zero.
**** The duration on China’s visible holdings of
Treasuries is a bit above the norm for reserve
managers, and China holds a ton of U.S. Agency
bonds (MBS), so it should get an above average

return on its dollar reserves. SAFE isn’t particularly
risk averse.
***** I would like to think my efforts here had an
impact on SAFE, if by nothing else making folks
aware that there was an issue. But in reality, SAFE
appears to be adjusting the BOP goods line to limit
reported errors, and as the renminbi stabilized,
settlement conversion increased, and the state banks
started adding more to their financial assets, SAFE

needed to report a higher current account surplus to
map to the larger observed buildup of Chinese assets
abroad.

聯友金屬 7610 TT (綠能環保) 
供應跟不上需求擴大 
投資與評價 
1. 3Q26 受惠國際鎢供應持續吃緊、鎢價維持高檔，加上屏東二廠新增
30%產能陸續開出，自售比重持續提升，預估營收季增 47.5%，毛利率
66.5%，EPS 27.32 元創單季新高。 
2. 2027 年成長動能由鎢價轉向擴產與產品高值化，屏南三廠、屏科廠陸
續開出，產品由鎢酸鈉延伸至 APT、氧化鎢、鎢粉及碳化鎢，自售比重
有機會挑戰 60%，預估 2026/27 EPS 83.77/109.27 元。 
3. 目前股價評價位於近兩年本益比 10～24 倍區間中段，考量非中鎢供應
鏈稀缺性、自售比重提升及 2027 年新產能接棒，給予區間中間偏上的
22 倍 FY27E PER，給予強力買進評等，目標價 2,400 元(22x FY27E 
EPS)。

重點更新 
3Q26 鎢價與自售業務推升獲利續增 
預估 3Q26 營收 28.43 億元（+47.5% QoQ） ，主要受惠國際鎢價維持高檔、
鎢酸鈉自售業務成長，以及屏東二廠新增 30%產能陸續開出；毛利 18.90 億
元（+44.8% QoQ） ，毛利率66.5%（-1.3ppts QoQ） ，營收與毛利仍維持高
速成長 。 營業費用1.88 億元 （+55.6% QoQ） ， 稅後淨利13.27 億元 （+40.3% 
QoQ），EPS 27.32 元創單季新高。預估 4Q26 隨新產能進一步量產、自售比
重過半，營收29.59 億元 （+4.1% QoQ） ，惟費用投入增加，EPS 降至 26.17
元。 
2027 年產能與高值化產品接棒 
預估 2027 年營收 132.79 億元 （+52.1% YoY） ，成長動能由鎢價逐步轉向產
能擴張及產品高值化，屏南三廠與屏科廠新產能陸續開出，產品由鎢酸鈉延
伸至 APT、氧化鎢、鎢粉及碳化鎢，自售比重有機會挑戰 60%；毛利 87.22
億元（+48.9% YoY） ，毛利率65.7%（-1.4ppts YoY） 。惟新廠與新工序投入
增加 ， 營業費用20.62 億元 （+202.8% YoY） ， 使營業利益66.60 億元 （+28.7% 
YoY） 、 營益率50.2%（-9.1ppts YoY） ； 稅後淨利53.09 億元 （+30.4% YoY），
EPS 109.27 元再創年度新高。後續營運重點將轉向新產能利用率，以及高值
化產品的提升速率。

### Page 2

訪談內容 
3Q26 鎢價與自售業務推升獲利續增 
預估 3Q26 營收 28.43 億元（+47.5% QoQ） ，主要受惠國際鎢供應持續吃緊、鎢價維持高檔，
加上鎢酸鈉自售業務成長 ， 以及屏東二廠增加的30%新產能部分陸續開出 ； 國際市場中國持續限
制鎢相關產品出口，尤其對美國與日本出口限制趨嚴，使國際客戶加速建立非中供應鏈，聯友以
二次硬質合金回收生產鎢產品，在競爭者有限的情況下，可望維持穩定的需求與產品售價。預估
毛利 18.90 億元（+44.8% QoQ） ，毛利率66.5%（-1.3ppts QoQ） ，雖自售產品比例提高，但
毛利率較 2Q26 高基期略降 ； 營業費用1.88 億元 （+55.6% QoQ） ， 營業利益17.02 億元 （+43.7% 
QoQ） ，營益率59.9%（-1.6ppts QoQ） 。預估稅後淨利13.27 億元 （+40.3% QoQ），EPS 27.32
元創歷史新高。除鎢產品外，公司亦持續推進循環再製鈷粉客戶認證與出貨，作為後續產品高值
化方向。

圖 1：聯友金屬獲利預估

資料來源: 國泰證期研究部整理

4Q26 營收續創高峰 
預估 4Q26 營收 29.59 億元（+4.1% QoQ） ，從上季大幅成長後仍維持季增，主要受惠鎢產品自
售業務持續成長，以及預期國際鎢供需仍偏緊，且公司新產能進一步進入量產階段，使單季自售
比重過半 。 中國掌握全球主要原生鎢供應 ， 出口限制使海外供應鏈短期難以快速補足缺口 ， 軍工 、
半導體及 AI 相關精密加工需求則持續支撐國際鎢價。預估毛利 19.21 億元（+1.7% QoQ） ，毛
利率 64.9%（-1.5ppts QoQ） ；惟營業費用增至3.00 億元（+59.6% QoQ） ，在於新的工序流程
投資持續投入，使營業利益 16.22 億元（-4.7% QoQ） ，營益率54.8%（-5.1ppts QoQ） 。預估
稅後淨利 12.71 億元（-4.2% QoQ），EPS 26.17 元；整體而言 4Q26 營收與毛利仍向上，但費
用增加使獲利較 3Q26 高峰略為回落。

### Page 3

2026 年鎢價大漲與自售比例增加 
預估 2026 年營收 87.31 億元 （+521.2% YoY） ，受惠國際鎢價大幅上漲、鎢酸鈉自售業務放量，
以及非中鎢供應鏈需求快速提升。中國鎢出口限制使國際市場供給趨緊，聯友具備廢硬質合金回
收、濕法冶金及既有海外客戶基礎，使鎢價上漲透過自售產品，直接轉化為獲利成長。預估毛利
58.57 億元 （+1,254.0% YoY） ， 毛利率由2025 年的 30.8%大幅提升至 67.1%（+36.3ppts YoY），
主因仍在產品國際報價及自售業務成長明顯；營業費用 6.81 億元（+452.3% YoY） ，營業利益
51.76 億元（+1,573.7% YoY） ，營益率59.3%（+37.3ppts YoY） 。預估稅後淨利40.70 億元
（+1,587.1% YoY），EPS 83.77 元創歷史新高，2026 年為公司材料處理量由加工大幅轉向自售
產品後，獲利結構明顯轉變。

圖 2：鎢金屬價格走勢

\

資料來源：Fastmarkets, 國泰證期研究部整理

2027 年產能與高值化產品接棒 
預估 2027 年營收 132.79 億元（+52.1% YoY） ，在2026 年鎢價與自售業務快速成長後，2027
年成長動能逐步由價格轉向產能擴張及產品高值化。公司規劃由既有鎢酸鈉進一步向APT、氧化
鎢、鎢粉及碳化鎢延伸，屏南三廠與屏科廠相關新產能預計陸續開出，鎢相關加工產能有機會擴
增倍翻，若產品認證與量產順利，可望提高公司在鎢產業鏈的垂直整合程度。預估毛利87.22 億
元（+48.9% YoY） ，毛利率65.7%（-1.4ppts YoY） ，在營收快速擴張下仍維持六成以上高毛利
率；惟配合新廠、新產品及營運規模擴張，營業費用增至 20.62 億元（+202.8% YoY） ，使營業
利益 66.60 億元（+28.7% YoY） ，營益率降至50.2%（-9.1ppts YoY） 。預估稅後淨利53.09 億
元（+30.4% YoY），EPS 109.27 元再創年度新高；2027 年營收與毛利仍維持高速成長，但費用
投入增加使獲利增速低於營收，後續營運重點將轉向新產能利用率，及APT、氧化鎢、鎢粉、碳
化鎢等高值化產品的提升速率。

### Page 4

非中鎢供應鏈稀缺性提升 
中國鎢出口限制使國際供應持續偏緊，聯友藉二次硬質合金回收與濕法冶金技術，受惠國際客戶
加速建立非中供應鏈，2026 年鎢價上漲、自售比重提升及屏東二廠新增產能開出，帶動獲利明
顯成長；2027 年成長動能則由格進一步轉向產能擴張與產品高值化，隨屏南三廠及屏科廠新產
能陸續開出，產品由鎢酸鈉延伸至 APT、氧化鎢、鎢粉及碳化鎢，自售部分有機會在 2027 年挑
戰 60%。預估 2026/27 EPS 為 83.77/109.27 元。3. 目前股價評價位於近兩年本益比 10～24
倍區間中段，考量非中鎢供應鏈稀缺性、自售比重提升及 2027 年新產能接棒，給予區間中間偏
上的 22 倍 FY27E PER，給予強力買進評等，目標價 2,400 元(22x FY27E EPS)。

產業概況 
鎢金屬需求持續由工業與國防驅動 
2026 年中國鎢總供給約 8.51 萬噸 W、YoY +12.4%，其中原生礦約 4.36 萬噸、幾乎零成長，回
收鎢則由高價刺激增加約 50%至約 3.0 萬噸 ， 使回收料占供給由2025 年的 25–26%升至約 35%；
換言之 2026 幾乎所有可快速增加的供給彈性都來自回收處理，使得非新礦山供應的競爭也明顯
升高。顯示高鎢價已開始抑制價格敏感度較高的傳統用途，但切削工具、航太與國防等剛性需求
仍維持成長。全球鎢需求主要仍來自硬質合金，應用於切削工具、礦山鑽頭、汽車與精密加工；
其中礦業與營建約占 2025 年全球 WC 市場 38.5%。 
國防需求的重要性在 2026 年進一步提高，美國與以色列對伊朗軍事行動、俄烏戰爭持續消耗飛
彈與穿甲彈用鎢，這類用途與硬質合金不同，使用後無法回收，使其對原生鎢形成永久性消耗。
因全球每年鎢需求約 10～11 萬噸，其中原生礦約占 65%、回收料約 35%，現有需求須依賴二
次資源補足。短期新礦與冶煉產能建置時間長，因此即使高價刺激新增供給，供需緊張仍不容易
快速逆轉。 
各應用來看硬質合金(Cemented carbide)佔比最大，主要用於切削刀具、採礦鑽頭、耐磨零件與
國防彈藥等。加工材/材加工品（Mill products） ：如鎢絲、鎢棒、鎢板等。鋼鐵與合金（Steel & 
alloys） 用於高速鋼、耐熱合金鋼等。化學品 （Chemicals） ：催化劑、顏料及化學試劑。其中 PCB
鑽針需求在近年也快速增加，據估計因 AI server PCB 變得更厚、更多層、更難鑽，高速低損耗
材料使 WC 鑽針磨耗大幅提高。AI server PCB 已朝 28–46 層甚至更高層數發展，而一般鑽針
原本約可加 工 3,000 孔 ， 在高階材料下可能降到約600 孔 ， 在相同鑽孔量可能需要接近4–5 倍
鑽針消耗。半導體需求在 4Q26 仍然偏強，其中第一類是半導體製程本身使用的高純度鎢/WF₆
等材料，另一類是晶圓、封裝、設備與零組件加工所使用的 WC 超硬工具。整體而言隨著 AI 
GPU/ASIC、HBM 與先進封裝擴產，使高純度 W 與精密 WC 工具需求維持穩定成長。  
圖 3：鎢金屬主要應用

資料來源：S&P Global, 國泰證期研究部整理

### Page 5

中國七成產能限制鎢出口影響大 
全球鎢供應高度集中，在2025 年全球鎢精礦開採產量約 8 萬多噸，中國占約75%，且中國同時
掌握從礦山、APT、鎢粉到碳化鎢的完整加工鏈，鎢的供應風險除了礦源集中，更包含中游精煉
能力集中 。2H25 開始中國開始收緊鎢礦開採配額並實施出口管制後 ， 非中國市場供給明顯轉緊 ，
價格也顯著提升。 
全球鎢市場主要依賴中國供應，因此中國價格大致主導全球定價；但出口限制與供應鏈安全政策
出現後，國際買家開始建立中國以外的礦源、冶煉及回收體系，使得國際報價持續走高，鎢回收
目前約占全球供給三成，雖然產地來源風險較低，但是既有供應鏈的產能瓶頸，使得回收流程取
得鎢的價格持續走高。雖陸續有些新礦場宣告將投入開發，但仍需兩年以上時間準備，舊有礦場
的重啟也面臨到回收率與經濟性的挑戰，且對於環境衝擊高未必適合歐美國家，目前最為環保與
的作法仍是從回收鎢開始加工。透過廢鎢回收陸續轉化為鎢酸鈉、APT、氧化鎢、鎢粉、碳化鎢，
將有利於賣方市場的精鍊供應商，邁向更高值化的末端產品發展。

圖 4：鎢礦蘊藏分布

資料來源：USGS, 國泰證期研究部整理

美國積極多元化鎢礦來源 
3Q26 美國政府承諾向 Elmet Group 投入 US$450mn，並搭配 DLA 最高 US$2bn 鎢戰略庫存
採購，政策目標已從單純取得礦源轉向礦山+APT/精煉+零組件的垂直整合。US$450mn 投資將
用於擴張美國鎢製造能力 、 取得長期原料 、 投資美國及盟國礦山與建立新的refining/conversion
能力；其中初期資金約 US$200mn。另一方面 DLA 與 Elmet 簽訂最高 US$2bn IDIQ 合約，最
低保證採購 US$150mn，品項直接涵蓋鎢礦、鎢精礦及鎢酸鈉；Elmet 待新增礦山、offtake 與
processing capacity 形成後才向戰略庫存交貨，以避免政府囤貨擠壓既有民間供應。 EQ 
Resources 規劃向美國供應 4,000 噸鎢精礦；但 Nevada APT processing facility全面恢復營運
預計要到 2H28，因此 4Q26 政策首先增加的是戰略採購與長約需求，而非立即增加APT 現貨供
給。這代表短期甚至可能強化非中國的原料競爭，但預計要數年後後才有機會逐步改善美國中游
加工缺口。

### Page 6

鈷金屬供給仍有短缺風險 
2025 年全球鈷礦產量約 31 萬噸，其中剛果民主共和國約23 萬噸、占七成比重，全球約 50%儲
量亦集中於剛果 ， 而中國又掌握全球七成以上鈷精煉能力 ， 因此上游礦源與中游精煉均高度集中 。
需求方面 2025 年全球鈷消費動力電池占比超過 65%，EV 仍是最大需求來源；雖電池高鎳化、
低鈷化以及 LFP 滲透率提升，會限制單位電池鈷用量成長，但 IT 電池的使用仍以氧化鈷為主要
材料。而長期更值得注意的是航空發動機、燃氣渦輪、國防設備所使用的高溫合金，以及硬質合
金與高性能磁鐵需求。高溫合金目前約占鈷需求 15～20%，工業工具、硬質合金與催化劑約一
成，使鈷逐漸從單一新能源金屬轉變成兼具新能源與戰略材料屬性的關鍵金屬；在供應集中及可
追溯要求提高下，循環再生鈷、高純度鈷粉與非中國精煉能力的戰略價值亦將持續提高。

公司簡介 
聯友金屬不依賴原生鎢礦，而是以廢硬質合金、鎢鋼及研磨屑等二次料作為鎢來源，經濕式冶金
回收後製成鎢酸鈉（Sodium Tungstate） ，同時回收鈷，形成WC/鎢鋼廢料、鎢酸鈉、APT、氧
化鎢的城市礦山供應鏈。公司目前鎢酸鈉年產能約 6,000 噸，2025 年鎢酸鈉出貨約 4,728 噸、
YoY +8%，其中代工約占 52%、自售約 48%，相較 2024 年代工 42%、自售 58%，可看出自
有設備利用率已處相對高檔 ； 公司規劃透過新廠將鎢酸鈉產能進一步提高至約 12,000 噸/年、較
現有產能增加 100%， 若以鎢酸鈉Na₂WO₄·2H₂O 含鎢重量約 55.7%換算 ， 滿產相當於一年可處
理約 6,700 噸 W contained。公司並規劃進一步向下游 APT 與氧化鎢延伸，使產品由目前以鎢
酸鈉為主，逐步建立回收料、鎢酸鈉、APT、WO₃的垂直整合能力。

### Page 7

ESG 政策 
公司在社會面評分上屬中上，然而需在經濟與環境面與揭露面強化:  
經濟面: 股東權益方面，公司優點為董事長親自出席股東常會；股東會結束後公開揭露逐項議案
之詳細表決資訊。公司可加強處為當年度未召開 2 次以上法人說明會。 
法規遵循方面，公司於題項評估期間未有不合理或受主管機關處分之情事。獨立董事方面，公司
優點為至少 2 名獨立董事之連續任期不超過 9 年。公司可加強處為獨立董事席次未達董事席次
二分之一以上。董事會永續治理方面，公司優點為有設置永續發展相關委員會；有制訂環境、社
會及治理之中長期發展策略；當年度永續報告書經董事會通過。公司可加強處為負責永續發展之
最終負責人非董事長或總經理。董事會績效與薪酬方面，公司優點為將董事績效評估與薪資報酬
連結。公司可加強處為企業永續績效尚未與高階主管及董事薪酬進行連結。 
董事會多元性方面，公司優點為揭露董事會多元化政策並揭露落實情形；董事會成員具備不同專
業背景 。 公司可加強處為任一性別董事席次未達1/3。 永續創新方面 ， 公司優點為取得綠色認證 。
公司可加強處為未揭露綠色相關部門設置情形與職責；未揭露綠色/永續採購之政策或金額。 
此外，產業創新方面，公司優點為對其專利之智慧財產權進行管理與保護，並揭露量化績效。 
法規遵循方面，公司近 3 年並未受公平交易委員會懲罰。

環境面: 環境系統與治理方面，公司優點為積極將綠色概念融入營運過程，打造綠色價值鏈；榮
獲 ISO 14001 環境管理認證；將環境管理作為與聯合國永續發展目標(SDGs)連結。空氣管理方
面，公司優點為制定空氣管理政策、揭露空氣汙染排放數據；公司可加強處為未揭露量化空氣汙
染減量目標。法規遵循方面，評估期間未違反空氣汙染相關法規或已針對違反情形提出具體改善
措施。氣候變遷管理方面，公司可加強處為未說明董事會或管理階層對氣候變遷議題之監督頻率
或事項；未揭露使用具公信力的 1.5 °C 以下及 3 °C 以上溫度情境評估氣候風險；未揭露與供
應鏈或合作夥伴共同推動因應氣候變遷相關策略；未揭露氣候變遷對公司所產生的機會項目。 
溫室氣體管理方面，公司優點為揭露至少三年的溫室氣體範疇一與二排放量，並揭露涵蓋範圍或
佔營收比率。公司可加強處為未揭露溫室氣體管理專責單位及相關管理政策；未揭露範疇三排放
量；未揭露量化溫室氣體減量目標；未揭露是否通過碳盤查認證。節能管理方面，公司優點為揭
露用電或節能數據。公司可加強處為未揭露能源管理專責單位及相關管理政策；未揭露節能量化
目標；未揭露能源風險對營運的影響評估結果。再生能源管理方面，公司可加強處為未揭露再生
能源管理專責單位及相關推動政策；未揭露再生能源使用量或發電量；未揭露量化再生能源發展
目標。廢水管理方面，公司優點為制定廢水管理政策、揭露廢污水排放量；公司可加強處為未揭
露量化廢汙水減量目標。 
揭路面: ESG 揭露方面，公司優點為依據 GRI 準則編寫永續報告書；於永續報告書清楚說明揭露
範疇；建置英文網站，並包含財務、業務及公司治理相關資訊；於永續報告書揭露重大性分析細
節。公司可加強處為永續報告書未依據 SASB 進行對應揭露；未上傳英文版永續報告書於公開資
訊觀測站；永續報告書尚未經過第三方機構確信或保證；未於官網揭露利害關係人問卷，供利害
關係人回饋；未說明公司營運對應SDGs 目標之具體作為；未揭露使用雙重重大性分析重大性議
題。

◼ 貨幣政策與殖利率： 聯準會言論放鷹是為重塑
公信力，9月按兵不動仍為較高機率情境，然不
能排除聯準會為了強化政策訊號而進行 「象徵
性升息 」的可能性 。推演美債長天期殖利率
2H26偏高表現、1H27適度回落。

揚壓抑股市估值但研判為暫時性 ，高殖利率信
用債在緩升息環境下表現仍穩健 ，惟債務風險
未緩解，利率敏感度高的長債價格波動較大。
熱門議題/資產影響推估 市場焦點
➢ 8月以來全球公債殖利率上升：美國10年期公債利
率上探4.8%，30年期公債殖利率創下 2007年6月
以來新高，日本10年期公債殖利率創下 1996年以
來新高。主要受財政壓力 、地緣不確定性 、貨幣
政策等多重因素帶動。
➢ 推演殖利率 2H26偏高表現、1H27適度回落：本
行預估聯準會 2026年尚不至採取升息 ，然鷹派言
論將讓高利率環境短期難解 ，有待2027年通膨壓

力緩解，利率方能適度回落。

©  CTBC
美國10年期公債殖利率站上4.8%，股市情緒受到影響但未失控
美債殖利率曲線短端因反應通膨擔憂而上升
盤勢回顧
股市情緒受到影響但未失控，信用債普遍收紅
3
• 升息擔憂推升 10年期公債殖利率站上 4.8%，但信用債過去一個月總報酬仍為正值 ：聯準會主席華許在8月底
Jackson Hole央行年會釋出鷹派訊號，市場提高對9月升息的押注，再加上近期中東地緣政治風險又再推動油價
上漲，美國10年期公債殖利率站上4.8%整數關卡，短天期利率主要反映聯準會升息預期升幅高於長天期利率。利
率走升雖對信用債帶來壓力，但利差收斂及息收仍為總報酬帶來貢獻，過去一個月美國非投資級債及新興主權
債仍上漲0.5%，就連長天期公司債亦仍收紅。
• 股市情緒受到影響但未失控：華許於7月底FOMC會議表示通膨有進展，然8月偏鷹派表述之後股市情緒受到一
定程度影響，不過修正幅度不大，並未出現恐慌情緒。而長天期利率其實在 8月初就開始出現上行趨勢 ，不過
多數指數8月以來仍收正報酬，僅利率敏感性較強的地產和公用事業表現較為落後。

資料來源：左， Bloomberg ，2026/9/2，右，Bloomberg，2026/9/2。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/9/2

©  CTBC
「財政壓力+地緣不確定性+AI支出 」= 高利率環境短期難解
期限溢酬+升息預期共同推升殖利率居高不下
環境分析
資料來源：(左) NY Fed ，2026/9；(右)中國信託商業銀行編繪，2026/6。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/8
• 財政可持續性疑慮 +地緣衝突頻繁，推升風險溢價要求：觀察聯準會經濟學家的 ACM模型可發現 ，期限溢價
(承擔未來利率變動、通膨與供給風險所要求的額外補償報酬 )達80個基點，也是去年以來美債殖利率走勢和降
息趨勢背離的主因。其中，近年地緣政治風險不確定性提高，以及主要國家推動大規模財政政策，使得財政與
債務可持續性疑慮不斷升溫等結構性因素，是市場對持有長端債券要求的風險貼水不斷增加。
• AI資本支出進一步推升中性利率水準：短期間，AI浪潮帶動科技巨頭大幅增加資料中心、AI晶片、電力基礎設
施及雲端設備投資，使企業資本支出需求顯著上升，推升整體資金需求，帶動實質利率走揚。長期而言，隨著
AI帶動生產力的顯著提升，增強了企業投資報酬率與經濟體的潛在成長率，經濟便能承受較高利率水準，促使

市場上修中性利率(r*)預估。

### Page 5

©  CTBC
聯準會放鷹重塑公信力，核心目的仍為穩定長天期利率
升息預期降溫時期，長天期利率卻不降反升
事件說明
通膨已見高峰，然需待2Q27方見明顯下滑
• 華許放鷹，重燃升息預期：聯準會主席華許在7月底FOMC會議表示通膨有進展，8月公布的數據也驗證該看法，
升息預期一度大幅降溫，但8月底JacksonHole他卻展現相當鷹派的論調，9月升息的機率再度回升到50%以上。
• 鴿派論述無法壓制長天期利率，聯準會轉換策略：市場近期呈現一種較特殊的現象，當升息預期下降、短端利
率回落時，長天期公債殖利率卻不降反升，甚至迫使財政部透過公債回購政策來緩和壓力。這可能反映市場擔
憂聯準會過早轉向鴿派，進而削弱其長期抗通膨的公信力，促使部分投資人減持長天期美債。在此背景下，本
行認為華許轉趨鷹派的重要目的之一，是維持市場對聯準會抗通膨決心的信任。若9月公布的8月通膨數據符合預
期，9月FOMC按兵不動仍為較高機率情境。在聯準會維持鷹派但不升息的情境下，本行預估下半年美國10年期
公債殖利率的區間將處於4.5%-4.8%，美國30年期公債殖利率則在5.1%-5.5%之間。

資料來源：左， Bloomberg ，2026/9，右，高盛，2026/9。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/9

進入緊縮周期的機率低，短線影響股債行情但不影響趨勢
推演殖利率2H26偏高表現、1H27適度回落
貨幣政策
過往在景氣高峰下利率上揚，本益比多半承壓
• 進入緊縮周期的機率低：雖然不能排除聯準會為了強化政策訊號與維持公信力，而進行「象徵性升息」，也就是
短期的政策微調。但以目前的通膨與就業數據趨勢來看，並不會是新一輪升息循環的起點。通膨壓力在2027上
半年預估將有較明顯的緩解，屆時聯準會的立場將再轉換。
• 聯準會若升息，10年期利率或先上探5%，暫時壓抑股市估值：推演後續路徑，長天期殖利率將呈現2H26偏高
表現，1H27殖利率適度回落的格局。但若聯準會2H26升息，市場第一時間將因擔憂持續性升息的可能，而使
美國10年期公債殖利率先上探 5%。從長週期角度而言，利率與景氣及股市評價走勢亦步亦趨 ，不過當景氣處
於高峰時利率上揚，本益比多半承壓，因為景氣高峰意味企業獲利再成長空間有限，或股價已經被充分評價。
考量AI基礎建設資本支出週期尚未結束，企業獲利仍保持兩位數成長下，利率上揚對估值的壓抑研判為暫時性。

資料來源：左， 中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/9，右，Bloomberg，2026/9。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/9

©  CTBC
日本債匯承壓，利差交易平倉與干預日圓皆對美債形成干擾風險
外部風險
資料來源：(左)Bloomberg，2026/8；(右)日本央行，2026/9。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/9
• 《日本成長戰略》+史上最大預算案，日債匯市面臨拋售壓力：5月日本政府擬推動規模達370兆日圓的《日本
成長戰略》後，市場對財政可持續性的疑慮快速升溫，5~8月內外資合計賣超20兆日圓日本公債；隨後日本政
府提出2027財年預算達到143兆日圓、年增16%，加上美國持續對日本升息施加壓力，日本利匯率持續承壓。
• 日本如暴力升息或引發利差交易平倉衝擊美債，然債務壓力限制下機率不高：今年美日利差在日本貨幣政策正
常化後罕見擴大，利差交易餘額(日本金融機構轉移海外資產 )較過去兩年明顯增加，若日本升息過快或日圓快
速升值，觸發利差交易平倉至美債部位減碼影響較大。同時，若日圓貶值壓力失控，財務省加大出售美元資產
進行匯市干預，可能增加美債賣壓推升殖利率，加劇金融市場波動。然美國已設立FIMA Repo機制避免日本大

量賣出美債；而日本債務壓力承重、升息速度受限，加以美債利率維持高檔，短期利差交易大量平倉誘因有限。

©  CTBC
利率敏感度高的長債波動上升，然基本面穩健支撐股債市
2016年聯準會升息循環未對信用債帶來嚴重衝擊
投資觀點
資料來源：(左) Bloomberg、BofA，2026/4/7；(右) Bloomberg，2026/9/1 。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/9/1
• 高殖利率信用債在緩升息環境下表現仍穩健：公債利率持續反映升息預期，市場擔憂信用債出現如2022年般重
挫，然而貨幣政策不變仍為目前基本情境，再者，每次升息大環境不同，美國通膨已見觸頂向下、也不像2022
年時嚴峻，就算升息應只是短暫政策調整 ，信用債表現預期較類似2015年緩升息循環：美 10年期公債利率緩
升，經濟成長持穩支撐信用債上漲。預期在企業獲利及景氣成長循環未結束前，非投資級債這類高殖利率資產
在穩健債信支持下波動可望降低，惟債務風險未緩解，利率敏感度高的長債(尤其是25年以上)價格波動仍大。
• 短線利率反彈，雖然基本面支持輪動，但資金高度參與下須保留部位彈性：過去利率自波段低點回升期間，股
市多能同步上漲，反映殖利率上升通常與景氣及企業獲利改善有關。本次多了財政壓力的結構性因素，限縮未

來幾個月的指數空間，但企業獲利穩健成長的基本趨勢不變，資金並未撤出股市，而是轉向加快輪動布局速度，
投資上仍建議降低單一主題集中度，並且適度提高部位彈性，利用市場震盪時布局投資機會。
過去長天期利率反彈期間美股表現多偏正向

1
AI代理將推升科技獲利
分析師／張乘維、謝孟璇、張偉漢
研究範疇／科技
科技產業投資攻略
出 刊 日 : 2026/9/29
熱門議題 / 投資關鍵
《AI代理將持續推動科技與半導體獲利成長》
根據機構LSEG統計，受到AI代理推動GPU、記憶體、
客製化AI晶片，以及相關硬體獲利持續高成長 。機
構上調科技產業 4Q26、1Q27獲利年增率到 53.7%、
51.6%，本行樂觀看待科技產業發展後勢。
受到AI代理需求大增與大量低價的開放模型加入 ，
帶動2026年Token使用量呈現指數成長 。根據大型
語言模型 API聚合平台OpenRouter統計，週Token
使 用 量 由2026 年 2-3 兆 Tokens暴增至 9 月 129 兆

Tokens，短短9個月成長超過 40倍，顯示AI代理帶
動AI推論指數級擴張 。另外，Meta於9月發表個人
AI代理Muse應用軟體，可替用戶「管理郵件、訂餐
廳、填寫表格 、比價網購 、安排日程」，用戶關閉
軟體後會繼續完成任務 。預計也將有眾多企業在
Muse平台使用自己的 AI代理。機構統計Muse在開
放前12天美加 iOS下載量達 180萬，高於ChatGPT
同期的130萬，這代表AI推論需求仍將供不應求。

吸納量
重點摘要 《AI代理與推論將帶動2027年半導體成長三成》
受到AI持續推動對資料中心 、高效運算 GPU/CPU
與HBM、DRAM、NAND等記憶體強勁需求 。機
構WSTS統計2026年上半導體市場規模達到 7,020
億美元、成長102%。其中記憶體成長高達302%、
邏輯IC成長45%。所以WSTS於8月上調全球半導體
2026年銷售額到 1.66兆美元 、年增108%(機構6月
時預估 1.51兆美元 、年增90%)，也同步上調 2027
年銷售額至 2.14兆美元、年增29%(機構6月時預估
1.91兆美元、年增27%)。記憶體和邏輯IC合計將佔
83%的半導體市場。機構預估2027年半導體銷售額
仍將由AI資料中心需要的相關晶片帶動 ，包含記憶

體將年增36%、邏輯IC將年增21%。
《博通上修AI營收預估，雲服務商與AI模型商持續
加大投資客製化AI晶片》
博通FY26Q3( 2026/5-2026/7 )營收、EPS優於預
期，公司將2027財年包括客製化AI晶片( AI ASIC )
及AI網路的AI營收由1,000億美元上修至1,150億美
元，並預估2028財年AI營收將再翻倍至 2,300億美
元 ， 超越市場原先約 1,800 億 美 元 的 預 估 。
Anthropic和Google的TPU部署正按計畫推進 ，目
前博通已向Anthropic和Google大量交付了TPU v7
Ironwood，同時開始為 Google生產交付 TPU v8i，
預計本季將大幅提升交付 TPU v8i的規模。另外，
OpenAI和Meta的AI晶片專案也有所進展 ，公司已
推出OpenAI第一代客製化AI晶片Jalapeño。
• 機構上調2027年半導體市場
到年增29%
• 博通上修2027財年AI營收預
測，並預估2028財年AI營收

將翻倍至2,300億美元。

3
吸納量
重點摘要 上述及後續專案將共同為博通 2027、2028財年AI
營收目標提供支撐 ，博通對未來AI營收的預估也顯
示雲端業者及 AI模型商正持續增加客製化 AI晶片投
資金額 。在客戶的 AI晶片部署進度上 ，博通預計
Anthropic在2026年將部署1吉瓦Ironwood系統、
2027年部署5吉瓦TPU v8i、2028年部署10吉瓦AI
晶片系統 ，預計Anthropic在2027年開始將成為博
通 最 大 客 製 化AI 晶 片 客 戶 ； 此 外， 公 司 也 預 計
OpenAI在2027年將部署 1.3吉瓦 Jalapeño、2028
年部署超過 5吉瓦Jalapeño及後續客製化 AI晶片。
由於市場預估客戶每部署 1吉瓦AI晶片系統約可為

博通帶來150~250億美元營收，根據博通公布的客
戶部署量，未來AI營收仍有上修機會。
《邁威爾上調 2027及2028財年營收預測 ，因市場
對資料中心互連、網路擴展產品需求強勁》
由於市場對資料中心互連 、擴展、客製化產品需求
強勁，邁威爾也上調了2027財年( 2026/2-2027/1 )
及2028財年( 2027/2-2028/1 )營收預測，將2027
財年營收預測由原先 115億美元上調至 120億美元
( YoY+45% )、2028財年營收預測由原先165億美
元上調至180億美元( YoY+50% ) 。邁威爾2028財
年成長潛力來自網路業務規模擴大 ，包括1.6T光模
組DSP、交換器，以及光學互連等新興需求；微軟
Maia 300、與Google的合作以及 XPU附加產品也
推升了2028財年客製化業務營收，市場對邁威爾AI
網通及客製化晶片項目的投資持續增加。
• 市場預估客戶每部署 1吉瓦
AI晶片系統約可為博通帶來
150~250億美元營收 ，未來
博通AI營收仍有上修機會。

重點摘要
零售客流量表
4
• AI 正從概念驗證轉向落地，並
產生可衡量的商業成果
《AI 正從概念驗證轉向落地，並產生可衡量的商業
成果》
9 越多家軟體龍頭不約而同揭露代理式 AI (Agentic
AI) 已由概念驗證階段跨入實質營收貢獻。益華電腦
(Cadence) 指出，其新推出的代理層採「消費加訂
閱」全新計費模式，且歷史經驗顯示效率提升不會
壓縮底層工具用量，一如 2006 年推出速度快 10 倍
的模擬器後客戶用量不減反增，原因在於晶片複雜
度呈指數成長，依台積電技術藍圖，未來 5 年晶片
複雜度將暴增48倍，人力根本無法同比例擴充。賽
富時 (Salesforce) 則與 Anthropic 共同推出
Claudeforce，並揭露頂級整合方案定價 550 美元，

較傳統業務團隊分散採購多套軟體之 1,300 美元支
出下降逾五成。近期財報數據顯示賽富時客戶加速
採用 AI 代理，AI 代理工作量加速成長並驅動相關
營收成長。MongoDB 的 Voyage 嵌入模型透過
Claude Code、Codex 等程式碼代理主動推薦，成
為全新的免費導流入口，單季即新增 2,900家 淨新
客戶；同時 MongoDB 也開始扮演前沿實驗室推理
記憶層角色，年化營收已達 5 億美元的語音 AI 獨角
獸 ElevenLabs 即因原生雲端資料庫無法承載代理
規模而轉單。Snowflake 則觀察到企業導入代理式
編碼工具後，資料遷移時程由過去以年為單位大幅
壓縮至一季內完成，帶動其由時間人力計價轉向成
果制計價的商業模式轉型。

重點摘要
零售客流量表
5
• 資安已從單純的成本中心轉為
代理式AI落地的先決條件，預
算來源正從單一資安部門擴散
至整體 AI 治理預算。
《資安躍居代理式 AI 時代最確定的新稅基》
代理式 AI 普及的另一面，是攻擊面與治理風險同步
暴增，資安支出因此出現明確的預算擴張訊號。
CrowdStrike 執行長明確表示，客戶端此刻是「資
安部門首度替IT踩油門而非踩煞車」，公司並預估，
AI 基礎建設支出將由 2.7 兆美元擴大至 2030 年的
6 兆美元，AI 偵測與應變 (AIDR) 滲透率終將如當年
的防毒軟體一樣趨近 100%。Palo Alto Networks
則揭露，其利用多模型弱點掃描，六成弱點由自研
模型找出、三成靠 OpenAI、一成來自其他模型，

印證單一模型無法涵蓋所有攻擊面，企業勢必得靠
平台化廠商整合多重情報來源。執行長並直言，一
般企業平均仍在使用 30 至 40 套資安工具，資料無
法互通，正是 AI 得以強化既有龍頭護城河而非顛覆
產業的關鍵原因。ServiceNow 與 Google 則分別
從身分治理與弱點修復角度切入：前者將 7 億台已
納管設備擴大至涵蓋逾 40 億台新設備，導入「零
常設權限」概念因應代理身分的短暫性；後者透過
Wiz 加 CodeMender 形成掃描、修復、驗證的完
整閉環，帶動逾九成財星百大企業採用其資安服務。
資安已從單純的成本中心轉為代理式 AI 落地的先決
條件，預算來源正從單一資安部門擴散至整體 AI 治
理預算，具備海量原生遙測資料的平台型業者，將
持續享有資料規模帶來的護城河。
大摩基準情境預估網安市值 2028 達 3 千億美元，CAGR 23%
資料來源：摩根史坦利，2026/9/20，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/09/22
(市值，十億美元)

生産

企業景況感（日銀短観）
日銀短観6月調査の業況判断DI（「良い」－「悪い」）は、全産業で＋17ptと3月調査（＋15ｐｔ）から改善。非
製造業は悪化したが、製造業が改善した。業種別に見ると、製造業では、繊維、窯業・土石、鉄鋼などが
改善。非製造業は、建設、不動産、対事業所サービス、対個人サービスなどが悪化した。先行き（9月）に
ついては、製造業、非製造業ともに低下が見込まれている。
